          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">可以用于回归任务的决策树算法：</plain_text>

          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">(Classification and</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>

            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">Regression Tree)</plain_text>

    </shape>
    <shape tag="shape" id="27" name="TextBox 26" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.98" fontColor="#996600" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.98" fontColor="#996600" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">[</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.98" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.98" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">L. Breiman</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.98" fontColor="#996600" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.98" fontColor="#996600" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">, J. H. Friedman, R. A. Olshen, and C. J. Stone’s book</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#996600" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#996600" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">“Classification and Regression Trees” published by Wadsworth</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#996600" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#996600" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">in 1984]</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>

      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">基于决策树的最强大算法：</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">RF (Random Forest)</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>
    <shape tag="shape" id="29" name="TextBox 28" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#996600" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#996600" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">[</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">L. Breiman’s </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#996600" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Calibri" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#996600" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">MLJ’01 paper “Random Forest”]</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>
    <shape tag="shape" id="30" name="TextBox 29" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#008000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#008000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">这是一种“集成学习”方法 </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Wingdings" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#008000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Wingdings" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#008000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#008000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#008000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">第 </plain_text>
          </text_run>

        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="27.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="27.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">基本流程</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>
    <shape tag="shape" id="25" name="TextBox 24" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="24" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="24" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">策略：“分而治之”</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">(divide-and-conquer)</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>
    <shape tag="shape" id="26" name="TextBox 25" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">自根至叶的递归过程</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>
    <shape tag="shape" id="27" name="TextBox 26" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">在每个中间结点寻找一个“划分”</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">(split or test)</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">属性</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>
    <shape tag="shape" id="28" name="TextBox 27" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">三种停止条件：</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>
    <shape tag="shape" id="29" name="TextBox 28" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">(1) </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">当前结点包含的样本全属于同一类别，无需划分</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">;</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>
    <shape tag="shape" id="30" name="TextBox 29" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">(2) </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">当前属性集为空</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">, </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">或是所有样本在所有属性上取值相同，无法划分</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">;</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">(3) </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">当前结点包含的样本集合为空，不能划分</plain_text>
          </text_run>

    <shape tag="shape" id="4" name="矩形 3" presetGeometry="rect">

          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">剪枝</plain_text>

            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">—</plain_text>

          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#0000FF" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

    <shape tag="shape" id="5" name="矩形 4" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">验证集精度</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">“脐部</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">=?” </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">划分前</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">: </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#0000FF" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#0000FF" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">３</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">／７＝</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">42.9</plain_text>

        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="27.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="27.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">从“树”到“规则”</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="27.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="27.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">	</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Wingdings" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Wingdings" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none"> </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">一棵</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#FF0000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">决策树</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">对应于一个“规则集”</plain_text>
          </text_run>

        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

        </paragraph>

          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="" fontColor="#008000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="" fontColor="#008000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="" fontColor="#008000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">C4.5</plain_text>

      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">

            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="27.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">多变量</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">(multivariate)</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="27.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="27.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">决策树</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>
    <shape tag="shape" id="29" name="TextBox 28" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="21.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">多变量决策树：每个非叶结点不仅考虑一个属性</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>
    <shape tag="shape" id="30" name="TextBox 29" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">例如“</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#0000FF" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#0000FF" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">斜决策树</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">” </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="15.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="Times New Roman" fontSize="15.96" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">(oblique decision tree) </plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">不是为每个非叶结点寻找</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">最优划分属性，而是建立一个</plain_text>
          </text_run>
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#0000FF" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="20.04" fontColor="#0000FF" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">线性分类器</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>
    <shape tag="shape" id="31" name="TextBox 30" presetGeometry="rect">
      <text_box tag="text_box" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="24.02" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="24.02" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">更复杂的“混合决策树”甚至可以在结点</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
        <paragraph tag="paragraph" fontName="" fontSize="" fontColor="" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
          <text_run tag="text_run" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="24" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">
            <plain_text tag="plain_text" fontName="微软雅黑" fontSize="24" fontColor="#000000" bold="false" italic="false" underline="none">嵌入神经网络或其他非线性模型</plain_text>
          </text_run>
        </paragraph>
      </text_box>
    </shape>
